Abstract: Data scarcity and weak supervision continue to limit the performance of machine learning models in many real-world applications, such as mammography, where Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) often offers the best formulation. While recent foundation models provide strong semantic representations out of the box, effective augmentation of such representations of MIL data remains limited, as existing methods operate at the instance level and fail to capture intra-bag dependencies. In this work, we introduce SetFlow, a generative architecture that models entire MIL bags (i.e., sets) directly in the representation space. Our approach leverages the flow matching paradigm combined with a Set Transformer-inspired design, enabling it to handle permutation-invariant inputs while capturing interactions between instances within each bag. The model is conditioned on both class labels and input scale, allowing it to generate coherent and semantically consistent sets of representations. We evaluate SetFlow on a large-scale mammography benchmark using a state-of-the-art MIL-PF classification pipeline. The generated samples are shown to closely match the original data distribution and even improve downstream performance when used for augmentation. Furthermore, training on synthetic data alone shows competitive results, demonstrating the effectiveness of representation-space generative modeling for data-scarce and privacy-sensitive tasks.

</details>


### [76] [Matched-Learning-Rate Analysis of Attention Drift and Transfer Retention in Fine-Tuned CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16410)
*Ruize Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在匹配学习率条件下比较CLIP的Full Fine-Tuning和LoRA适配方法，发现LoRA在保持零样本迁移能力方面显著优于Full FT，且注意力漂移模式随学习率变化呈现系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP适配通常面临领域内精度提升与领域外迁移能力下降的权衡，而现有Full FT与LoRA的比较常因学习率约定不同而产生混淆。本研究旨在通过控制学习率变量，系统探究不同适配方法和优化尺度如何共同影响注意力漂移和迁移保持。

Method: 采用匹配学习率的对照实验设计，在CLIP ViT-B/32模型上对EuroSAT和Oxford-IIIT Pets数据集进行80次实验，涵盖4个共享学习率（10^-6、5×10^-6、10^-5、5×10^-5）和5个随机种子。评估指标包括注意力漂移度量、最佳验证精度和适配器感知的CIFAR-100零样本准确率。

Result: 学习率强烈调节结构变化：在EuroSAT上，Full FT从10^-6时的轻度熵增变为5×10^-5时的显著熵减，而LoRA在整个匹配网格中保持熵增。在匹配学习率下，LoRA保持的零样本迁移能力显著优于Full FT：EuroSAT上平均CIFAR-100准确率为45.13% vs 11.28%，Pets上为58.01% vs 8.54%。Oxford-IIIT Pets还揭示了机制效应：低学习率LoRA在领域内欠拟合。

Conclusion: 匹配学习率评估显著改变了Full FT与LoRA的比较解释。注意力漂移更适合作为表征保持的描述性诊断指标，而非迁移行为的因果解释。LoRA在保持零样本迁移能力方面具有明显优势，特别是在匹配学习率条件下。

Abstract: CLIP adaptation can improve in-domain accuracy while degrading out-of-domain transfer, but comparisons between Full Fine-Tuning (Full FT) and LoRA are often confounded by different learning-rate conventions. We study how adaptation method and optimization scale jointly shape attention drift and transfer retention in CLIP using a controlled matched-learning-rate comparison of Full FT and LoRA. The completed matrix contains 80 runs on CLIP ViT-B/32 across EuroSAT and Oxford-IIIT Pets, spanning four shared learning rates ($10^{-6}$, $5{\times}10^{-6}$, $10^{-5}$, $5{\times}10^{-5}$) and five seeds, and evaluates attention-drift metrics, best validation accuracy, and adapter-aware CIFAR-100 zero-shot accuracy. Learning rate strongly modulates structural change: on EuroSAT, Full FT moves from mild entropy broadening at $10^{-6}$ to marked contraction at $5{\times}10^{-5}$, whereas LoRA remains entropy-positive across the full matched grid. At matched learning rates, LoRA preserves substantially more zero-shot transfer than Full FT, averaging $45.13\%$ versus $11.28\%$ CIFAR-100 accuracy on EuroSAT and $58.01\%$ versus $8.54\%$ on Pets. Oxford-IIIT Pets also reveals a regime effect: low-learning-rate LoRA underfits in-domain, so method-only averages can obscure when LoRA becomes competitive. Supporting rollout, patch-to-patch, and CKA analyses are directionally consistent with the controlled matrix. Overall, matched-learning-rate evaluation materially changes the interpretation of Full FT versus LoRA, and attention drift is most useful as a descriptive diagnostic of representation preservation rather than a causal explanation of transfer behavior.

</details>


### [77] [Shifting the Gradient: Understanding How Defensive Training Methods Protect Language Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16423)
*Satchel Grant,Victor Gillioz,Jake Ward,Thomas McGrath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了两种防御性训练方法PPS和IP，发现它们通过不同机制防止LLMs获得"邪恶"特质：PPS能主动减少已有特质表达，而IP对已微调模型无效；机制上PPS通过梯度偏移实现防御，IP机制仍不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解PPS和IP这两种看似相似的防御性训练方法如何工作，以及它们是否通过相同机制防止大型语言模型获得不良特质。这两种方法都在训练期间向LLMs添加特质诱导对象，但具体机制尚不清楚。

Method: 采用行为比较和机制分析方法，以"邪恶"特质为案例研究。行为上测试两种方法在特质获取防御和已有特质减少方面的效果；机制上分析激活梯度变化、余弦相似性、梯度特征以及下一词预测损失等指标。

Result: 发现PPS和IP通过不同机制实现防御：1)行为上，PPS既能防御特质获取又能主动减少已有特质表达，而IP对已微调表达特质的模型无效；2)机制上，PPS沿PPS向量轴将激活梯度转向衰减方向，当与特质表达轴对齐时可逆转梯度压力，IP则具有不同的梯度特征且更分散，还会减少特质表达数据的下一词预测损失。

Conclusion: PPS和IP采用截然不同的防御机制，PPS通过梯度偏移机制有效防御特质获取并减少已有表达，而IP机制仍不明确，可能通过"解释掉"训练数据中的特质表达来运作。研究揭示了两种方法的机制差异，并提出了关于IP机制图景的开放性问题。

Abstract: Defensive training methods such as positive preventative steering (PPS) and inoculation prompting (IP) offer surprising results through seemingly similar processes: both add trait-inducing objects to large language models (LLMs) during training, and both defend the LLM against acquiring the trait. The surprising success of these methods comes with the question: how do they work? Are PPS and IP doing the same thing? We provide behavioral and mechanistic comparisons of these two methods using "evilness" as a case-study trait. Our central finding is that PPS and IP achieve their defensive benefits through distinct mechanisms. Behaviorally, we show that neither PPS nor IP operates through a purely associative mechanism; and PPS can both defend against trait acquisition and actively reduce pre-existing expression, whereas IP is ineffective in models that were previously finetuned to express the trait. This behavioral divergence is reflected mechanistically: PPS shifts the activation gradient towards an attenuating direction along the PPS vector axis. When the PPS vector is aligned with a trait-expressing axis, it can reverse the gradient pressure, reducing rather than increasing activation along that axis. In contrast, IP continues to resist a precise mechanistic account. Direct cosine similarity analyses reveal that IP has a characteristically different gradient signature than PPS, and qualitative analyses reveal IP's gradient to be more diffuse. Furthermore, IP reduces the next-token prediction loss on trait-expressing data where PPS need not, consistent with the notion that IP "explains away" the trait-expression in the training data. Taken together, our analyses reveal distinct mechanisms by which each method operates and highlight open questions about IP's mechanistic picture.

</details>


### [78] [Non-Stationarity in the Embedding Space of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16428)
*Jinmyeong Choi,Brad Shook,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨时间序列基础模型嵌入空间中非平稳性的可检测性，区分分布非平稳性（均值漂移、方差变化、趋势）与时间非平稳性（持续性），发现检测能力随偏移强度平滑下降且不同模型有特定失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将非平稳性与分布偏移混为一谈，模糊了经典时间序列分析和统计过程控制中的基本区别。SPC中非平稳性标志着过程离开稳定状态，检测这种偏离对质量监控和变点分析至关重要。研究旨在探索不同形式的分布非平稳性在TSFM嵌入空间中的线性可及性。

Method: 研究在受控条件下分析分布非平稳性（均值漂移、方差变化、线性趋势）在TSFM嵌入空间中的线性可及性，同时考察时间非平稳性（持续性，反映弱平稳性违反）。通过扫描偏移强度并探测多个TSFM，评估嵌入空间对非平稳性的检测能力。

Result: 研究发现嵌入空间对非平稳性的可检测性随偏移强度平滑下降，不同模型展现出特定于模型的失效模式。这表明TSFM在捕捉非平稳性方面存在局限性，且不同模型架构对不同类型的非平稳性敏感度不同。

Conclusion: 该研究澄清了TSFM嵌入空间中非平稳性的概念，区分了分布非平稳性和时间非平稳性，揭示了TSFM在检测非平稳性方面的系统局限性，为改进时间序列基础模型的鲁棒性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are widely used as generic feature extractors, yet the notion of non-stationarity in their embedding spaces remains poorly understood. Recent work often conflates non-stationarity with distribution shift, blurring distinctions fundamental to classical time-series analysis and long-standing methodologies such as statistical process control (SPC). In SPC, non-stationarity signals a process leaving a stable regime - via shifts in mean, variance, or emerging trends - and detecting such departures is central to quality monitoring and change-point analysis. Motivated by this diagnostic tradition, we study how different forms of distributional non-stationarity - mean shifts, variance changes, and linear trends - become linearly accessible in TSFM embedding spaces under controlled conditions. We further examine temporal non-stationarity arising from persistence, which reflects violations of weak stationarity due to long-memory or near-unit-root behavior rather than explicit distributional shifts. By sweeping shift strength and probing multiple TSFMs, we find that embedding-space detectability of non-stationarity degrades smoothly and that different models exhibit distinct, model-specific failure modes.

</details>


### [79] [(Sparse) Attention to the Details: Preserving Spectral Fidelity in ML-based Weather Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16429)
*Maksim Zhdanov,Ana Lucic,Max Welling,Jan-Willem van de Meent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mosaic是一个概率天气预测模型，通过功能扰动和块稀疏注意力机制解决ML天气预测中的频谱退化问题，在1.5°分辨率下实现最先进性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习天气预测中两个主要的频谱退化来源：(1)针对集合平均的确定性训练；(2)压缩编码造成的信息瓶颈

Method: 通过学习的函数扰动生成集合成员，使用块稀疏注意力机制在原生分辨率网格上操作，通过共享空间相邻查询的键和值以线性成本捕获长程依赖关系

Result: 在1.5°分辨率下，214M参数的Mosaic匹配或优于在6倍更细数据上训练的模型，在1.5°模型中达到最先进水平，产生校准良好的集合，其个体成员在所有解析频率上表现出近乎完美的频谱对齐

Conclusion: Mosaic通过创新的扰动机制和硬件对齐的注意力架构，有效解决了ML天气预测中的频谱退化问题，实现了高效的概率预测

Abstract: We introduce Mosaic, a probabilistic weather forecasting model that addresses two principal sources of spectral degradation in ML-based weather prediction: (1) deterministic training against ensemble means and (2) compressive encoding creating an information bottleneck. Mosaic generates ensemble members through learned functional perturbations and operates on native-resolution grids via block-sparse attention, a hardware-aligned mechanism that captures long-range dependencies at linear cost by sharing keys and values across spatially adjacent queries. At 1.5$°$ resolution with 214M parameters, Mosaic matches or outperforms models trained on 6 times finer data on headline upper-air variables and achieves state-of-the-art results among 1.5$°$ models, producing well-calibrated ensembles whose individual members exhibit near-perfect spectral alignment across all resolved frequencies. A 24-member, 10-day forecast takes under 12 seconds on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [80] [Sampling for Quality: Training-Free Reward-Guided LLM Decoding via Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16453)
*Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Wenhui Zhu,Bo Long,Zhipeng Wang,Suyash Gupta,Kayhan Behdin,Bee-Chung Chen,Deepak Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于概率框架的奖励引导解码方法，通过结合模型转移概率和前缀相关奖励势能来定义奖励增强的目标分布，无需训练即可在推理时提升序列质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解码方法主要优化token级似然而非序列级质量，存在局限性。需要一种无需训练、仅通过推理时采样就能提升序列质量的方法。

Method: 定义奖励增强的目标序列分布，结合模型转移概率和前缀相关奖励势能。开发序列蒙特卡洛算法，包括计算高效的前缀变体和匹配完整序列分布精确边缘的lookahead变体。集成重采样-移动更新和Metropolis-Hastings rejuvenation，支持块级生成。

Result: 在三个7B模型上取得显著提升：代码生成（HumanEval）提升达54.9%，超越最强采样基线9.1%-15.3%；数学推理（MATH500）提升达8.8%。Qwen2.5-7B在HumanEval上达到87.8%，MATH500上达到78.4%，持续优于强化学习方法GRPO。

Conclusion: 提出的概率框架为奖励引导解码提供了原则性方法，通过推理时采样显著提升序列质量，无需修改模型权重，在代码生成和数学推理任务上表现出色。

Abstract: We introduce a principled probabilistic framework for reward-guided decoding in large language models, addressing the limitations of standard decoding methods that optimize token-level likelihood rather than sequence-level quality. Our method defines a reward-augmented target distribution over complete sequences by combining model transition probabilities with prefix-dependent reward potentials. Importantly, the approach is training-free: it leaves model weights unchanged and instead modifies the inference distribution via reward potentials, with all gains arising purely from inference-time sampling. To sample from this distribution, we develop Sequential Monte Carlo algorithms, including a computationally efficient prefix-only variant and a lookahead variant whose intermediate targets match the exact marginals of the full sequence distribution. The framework also integrates resample-move updates with Metropolis-Hastings rejuvenation and supports block-wise generation, subsuming common decoding strategies such as temperature sampling and power-tempered objectives. Empirical results across three 7B models show significant gains. On code generation (HumanEval), our method improves base performance by up to 54.9% and surpasses the strongest sampling baselines by 9.1%-15.3%. On mathematical reasoning (MATH500), it achieves gains of up to 8.8%. Notably, it reaches 87.8% on HumanEval and 78.4% on MATH500 with Qwen2.5-7B, consistently outperforming the reinforcement learning method GRPO.

</details>


### [81] [DARLING: Detection Augmented Reinforcement Learning with Non-Stationary Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16684)
*Argyrios Gerogiannis,Yu-Han Huang,Venugopal V. Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARLING是一种用于分段平稳MDP的模型无关强化学习算法，无需先验知识即可处理任意次数的动态变化，在表格和线性MDP中均实现近乎最优的动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非平稳有限时域episodic MDP中的模型无关强化学习问题，重点关注分段平稳设置，其中奖励和转移动态可以任意次数变化，且无需先验知识。现有方法在动态遗憾界方面存在改进空间，需要开发更优算法。

Method: 提出DARLING（Detection Augmented Reinforcement Learning），这是一种模块化包装器，适用于表格和线性MDP。算法不依赖变化点知识，在满足变化点分离和可达性条件下，通过检测机制增强标准RL算法来处理非平稳性。

Result: DARLING改进了两种设置下的最佳可用动态遗憾界，并在标准基准测试中表现出优于现有方法的实证性能。同时建立了表格和线性MDP中PS-RL的首个极小极大下界，证明DARLING是首个近乎最优算法。

Conclusion: DARLING是处理分段平稳MDP的有效框架，无需变化点先验知识，在理论和实证上均优于现有方法，是首个被证明近乎最优的PS-RL算法。

Abstract: We study model-free reinforcement learning (RL) in non-stationary finite-horizon episodic Markov decision processes (MDPs) without prior knowledge of the non-stationarity. We focus on the piecewise-stationary (PS) setting, where both the reward and transition dynamics can change an arbitrary number of times. We propose Detection Augmented Reinforcement Learning (DARLING), a modular wrapper for PS-RL that applies to both tabular and linear MDPs, without knowledge of the changes. Under certain change-point separation and reachability conditions, DARLING improves the best available dynamic regret bounds in both settings and yields strong empirical performance. We further establish the first minimax lower bounds for PS-RL in tabular and linear MDPs, showing that DARLING is the first nearly optimal algorithm. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DARLING consistently surpasses the state-of-the-art methods across diverse non-stationary scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [Covariance-Based Structural Equation Modeling in Small-Sample Settings with $p>n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16894)
*Hiroki Hasegawa,Aoba Tamura,Yukihiko Okada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的因子结构方程模型估计方法，通过将协方差结构重新表述为自协方差和交叉协方差分量，结合似然可行集和相对误差约束，解决了小样本高维（p>n）情况下的估计稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于因子的结构方程模型（SEM）依赖基于似然的估计方法，要求样本协方差矩阵非奇异。但在小样本高维（p>n）情况下，样本协方差矩阵会变得奇异，导致传统方法失效。需要开发能够在小样本高维设置下稳定估计的新方法。

Method: 提出新的估计原理：将协方差结构重新表述为自协方差和交叉协方差分量。构建基于似然的可行集，并结合相对误差约束。该方法能够在p>n的小样本设置下进行稳定估计，特别关注结构参数的符号和方向恢复。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该方法在小样本高维设置下具有更好的稳定性，特别是在恢复结构参数的符号和方向方面表现优异。将基于协方差的SEM扩展到小样本设置，为决策提供了实用的方向性信息。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了传统SEM在小样本高维（p>n）情况下的估计问题，通过重新表述协方差结构和引入相对误差约束，实现了稳定估计，特别在恢复参数符号和方向方面具有优势，扩展了SEM的应用范围。

Abstract: Factor-based Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) relies on likelihood-based estimation assuming a nonsingular sample covariance matrix, which breaks down in small-sample settings with $p>n$. To address this, we propose a novel estimation principle that reformulates the covariance structure into self-covariance and cross-covariance components. The resulting framework defines a likelihood-based feasible set combined with a relative error constraint, enabling stable estimation in small-sample settings where $p>n$ for sign and direction. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show improved stability, particularly in recovering the sign and direction of structural parameters. These results extend covariance-based SEM to small-sample settings and provide practically useful directional information for decision-making.

</details>


### [83] [Positive-Only Drifting Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16519)
*Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PODPO是一种免似然、免梯度裁剪的生成式在线强化学习方法，通过优势加权局部对比漂移，仅使用正优势样本将动作导向高回报区域


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯策略和基于流的方法受限于单峰表达能力、复杂梯度裁剪或严格信任域要求，且都依赖对负样本的后验惩罚来纠正错误动作

Method: 利用漂移模型，通过优势加权局部对比漂移进行策略更新，仅使用正优势样本，利用生成模型的局部平滑性实现主动错误预防

Result: PODPO为在线环境中的生成式策略学习开辟了有前景的新途径

Conclusion: PODPO优雅地将动作导向高回报区域，同时利用生成模型的固有局部平滑性实现主动错误预防

Abstract: In the field of online reinforcement learning (RL), traditional Gaussian policies and flow-based methods are often constrained by their unimodal expressiveness, complex gradient clipping, or stringent trust-region requirements. Moreover, they all rely on post-hoc penalization of negative samples to correct erroneous actions. This paper introduces Positive-Only Drifting Policy Optimization (PODPO), a likelihood-free and gradient-clipping-free generative approach for online RL. By leveraging the drifting model, PODPO performs policy updates via advantage-weighted local contrastive drifting. Relying solely on positive-advantage samples, it elegantly steers actions toward high-return regions while exploiting the inherent local smoothness of the generative model to enable proactive error prevention. In doing so, PODPO opens a promising new pathway for generative policy learning in online settings.

</details>


### [84] [LASER: Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17224)
*Ege Çakar,Ketan Ali Raghu,Lia Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了递归架构（如TRMs）中激活轨迹的几何结构，发现其占据低维线性子空间，并提出了LASER压缩框架以节省内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管递归架构通过迭代潜在计算执行隐式推理，但这些推理轨迹的几何结构仍未被充分理解。需要探究递归展开过程中激活流形的特性，以理解权重共享如何影响计算轨迹。

Method: 研究TRMs在递归展开过程中的激活流形，发现激活占据有效线性低维子空间，其主方向可通过廉价幂迭代动态追踪。基于此提出LASER框架：通过矩阵无关的子空间追踪维护演化中的低秩基，并采用保真度触发的重置机制。

Result: 发现权重共享将迭代计算集中在少量主导特征方向上，且这种集中程度在不同计算位点差异显著。LASER框架实现了约60%的激活内存节省，且无统计显著的精度下降。

Conclusion: 递归架构在隐式推理过程中以特定方式分配表征能力，这种计算集中现象可被利用来提高潜在计算的效率和稳定性。研究提出了关于递归架构如何分配表征能力的基本问题。

Abstract: Recursive architectures such as Tiny Recursive Models (TRMs) perform implicit reasoning through iterative latent computation, yet the geometric structure of these reasoning trajectories remains poorly understood. We investigate the activation manifold of TRMs during recursive unrolling and find that activations occupy an effectively linear, low-dimensional subspace whose principal directions can be tracked dynamically with cheap power iterations. This suggests that weight-sharing concentrates iterative computation along a small number of dominant eigendirections, and we find that this concentration varies sharply across computational sites. We exploit this structure through LASER (Low-Rank Activation SVD for Efficient Recursion), a dynamic compression framework that maintains an evolving low-rank basis via matrix-free subspace tracking with a fidelity-triggered reset mechanism, achieving ${\sim}60\%$ activation memory savings with no statistically significant accuracy degradation. Our analysis raises questions about how recursive architectures allocate representational capacity during implicit reasoning, and whether this concentration can be exploited to improve the efficiency and stability of latent computation.

</details>


### [85] [SCATR: Simple Calibrated Test-Time Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16535)
*Divya Shyamal,Marta Knežević,Lan Tran,Chanakya Ekbote,Vijay Lingam,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCATR是一种轻量级BoN排序方法，通过少量校准数据从基础模型的隐藏表示中学习评分器，在测试时选择中实现精度与效率的良好平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放(TTS)通过并行生成多个候选响应并使用Best-of-N策略选择最佳响应来改进LLMs。现有方法中，学习型评分器(如PRMs)效果强但训练和运行成本高，而基于token对数概率的轻量级置信度启发式方法虽然便宜但性能较差。需要一种在轻量级启发式方法和强学习型评分器之间取得更好平衡的方法。

Method: SCATR(Simple Calibration for Test-time Ranking)方法：1) 使用少量校准数据集；2) 从基础模型的隐藏表示中学习轻量级评分器；3) 用于Best-of-N策略中的候选响应排序和选择。该方法避免了训练完整奖励模型的高成本，同时比简单的置信度启发式方法更有效。

Result: 在代码和数学推理基准测试中：1) 相比先前的基于置信度的基线方法，准确率提升高达9%；2) 相比在相同校准数据上的LoRA微调，达到相当准确率但可训练参数减少高达8000倍，计算成本显著降低；3) 训练和推理延迟分别降低高达150倍和1000倍；4) 与强PRM基线竞争，在某些场景下数学准确率提升7.8%，代码准确率提升4.2%，同时推理速度提升高达1000倍。

Conclusion: SCATR为可扩展的测试时选择提供了强大的精度-效率权衡，能够在保持轻量级的同时显著提升Best-of-N策略的效果，是测试时缩放中一种高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves large language models (LLMs) by allocating additional compute at inference time. In practice, TTS is often achieved through parallel scaling: generating multiple candidate responses and selecting the best via a Best-of-N (BoN) strategy. Its effectiveness therefore hinges on the scoring function. Learned scorers such as process reward models (PRMs) can be strong, but they are expensive to train and run. Lightweight confidence heuristics based on token log-probabilities are much cheaper, yet we find that they often perform substantially worse. To improve on lightweight confidence heuristics without incurring the full cost of stronger learned scorers, we introduce SCATR, a simple and efficient BoN ranking method that learns a lightweight scorer from a small calibration set using hidden representations from the base model. Across coding and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SCATR improves over prior confidence-based baselines by up to 9%. Relative to LoRA fine-tuning on the same calibration data, it achieves comparable accuracy with up to 8000x fewer trainable parameters and much lower compute, reducing training and inference latency by up to 150x and 1000x, respectively. SCATR is also competitive with strong PRM baselines, and in several settings improves accuracy by up to 7.8% on math and 4.2% on coding while enabling up to 1000x faster inference. Overall, SCATR offers a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off for scalable test-time selection.

</details>


### [86] [Diverse Dictionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17568)
*Yujia Zheng,Zijian Li,Shunxing Fan,Andrew Gordon Wilson,Kun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出多样化字典学习问题，在完全可识别性不可达的一般设置下，研究仍能保证恢复的内容和可普遍采用的偏置，证明即使没有强假设，与任意观测相关的潜在变量的交集、补集、对称差以及潜在到观测的依赖结构仍可识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常假设线性性或依赖辅助监督和函数约束，但这些假设在实践中很少可验证，且大多数理论保证在轻微违反下就会失效。为了在现实场景中使可识别性具有可操作性，研究在完全可识别性不可达的一般设置下，仍能保证恢复的内容和可普遍采用的偏置。

Method: 引入多样化字典学习问题来形式化这一观点。证明即使没有强假设，与任意观测相关的潜在变量的交集、补集、对称差以及潜在到观测的依赖结构仍可识别到适当的不可确定性程度。这些集合论结果可通过集合代数组合来构建隐藏世界的结构化基本视图。

Result: 当存在足够结构多样性时，这些结果进一步意味着所有潜在变量的完全可识别性。所有可识别性优势都来自估计过程中的简单归纳偏置，该偏置可轻松集成到大多数模型中。在合成和真实世界数据上验证了理论并展示了偏置的好处。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种互补视角，在完全可识别性不可达的情况下，通过集合论方法恢复潜在变量的结构化视图，并展示了简单归纳偏置如何提供可识别性保证，使可识别性在现实场景中更具可操作性。

Abstract: Given only observational data $X = g(Z)$, where both the latent variables $Z$ and the generating process $g$ are unknown, recovering $Z$ is ill-posed without additional assumptions. Existing methods often assume linearity or rely on auxiliary supervision and functional constraints. However, such assumptions are rarely verifiable in practice, and most theoretical guarantees break down under even mild violations, leaving uncertainty about how to reliably understand the hidden world. To make identifiability actionable in the real-world scenarios, we take a complementary view: in the general settings where full identifiability is unattainable, what can still be recovered with guarantees, and what biases could be universally adopted? We introduce the problem of diverse dictionary learning to formalize this view. Specifically, we show that intersections, complements, and symmetric differences of latent variables linked to arbitrary observations, along with the latent-to-observed dependency structure, are still identifiable up to appropriate indeterminacies even without strong assumptions. These set-theoretic results can be composed using set algebra to construct structured and essential views of the hidden world, such as genus-differentia definitions. When sufficient structural diversity is present, they further imply full identifiability of all latent variables. Notably, all identifiability benefits follow from a simple inductive bias during estimation that can be readily integrated into most models. We validate the theory and demonstrate the benefits of the bias on both synthetic and real-world data.

</details>


### [87] [Prior-Fitted Functional Flow: In-Context Generative Models for Pharmacokinetics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17670)
*César Ojeda,Niklas Hartung,Wilhelm Huisinga,Tim Jahn,Purity Kamene Kavwele,Marian Klose,Piyush Kumar,Ramsés J. Sánchez,Darius A. Faroughy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PFFFs：基于功能流的药代动力学生成基础模型，支持零样本群体合成和个体预测，无需手动参数调优


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决药代动力学中传统方法需要手动参数调优、难以处理稀疏不规则数据、缺乏不确定性校准的问题，实现零样本的群体合成和个体预测

Method: 学习功能向量场，显式地以整个研究群体的稀疏不规则数据为条件；构建开放获取文献语料库来指导先验；生成相干虚拟队列并预测部分观测的患者轨迹

Result: 在广泛的实际数据集上展示了最先进的预测准确性，能够生成具有校准不确定性的预测

Conclusion: PFFFs为药代动力学提供了一个强大的生成基础模型，实现了零样本群体合成和个体预测，具有校准的不确定性，在真实数据上表现优异

Abstract: We introduce Prior-Fitted Functional Flows, a generative foundation model for pharmacokinetics that enables zero-shot population synthesis and individual forecasting without manual parameter tuning. We learn functional vector fields, explicitly conditioned on the sparse, irregular data of an entire study population. This enables the generation of coherent virtual cohorts as well as forecasting of partially observed patient trajectories with calibrated uncertainty. We construct a new open-access literature corpus to inform our priors, and demonstrate state-of-the-art predictive accuracy on extensive real-world datasets.

</details>


### [88] [An Interpretable Framework Applying Protein Words to Predict Protein-Small Molecule Complementary Pairing Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16550)
*Jingke Chen,Jingrui Zhong,Tazneen Hossain Tani,Zidong Su,Xiaochun Zhang,Boxue Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PWRules框架通过可解释的蛋白质-小分子片段配对规则预测结合亲和力，在保持竞争力的同时提供结构洞察


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习模型在蛋白质-小分子结合预测中具有高准确性，但药物发现仍依赖蛋白质-配体相互作用原理和启发式方法。现有"黑盒"模型缺乏可解释性，限制了其在药物发现中的应用。

Method: 开发PWRules框架：1) 利用结合亲和力数据识别特权小分子片段；2) 通过可解释性模块定义这些片段与蛋白质"词"(语义序列单元)之间的互补配对规则；3) 使用PWScore函数对词-片段规则进行排序以优先选择活性化合物。

Result: 在基准数据集上，PWScore达到与物理模型(Glide)和深度学习模型(PSICHIC)相当的竞争性能，对训练集外的蛋白质靶点(如SARS-CoV-2主蛋白酶)具有广泛适用性。与现有方法集成时，PWScore捕获互补相互作用信息，产生更优的富集性能。结构分析表明，学习的词-片段规则在配体结合口袋附近显著富集，尽管训练过程没有明确的结构指导。

Conclusion: 通过提取和应用互补配对规则，PWRules为药物发现提供了一个可解释的框架，能够提供结构洞察并增强现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Despite the high accuracy of 'black box' deep learning models, drug discovery still relies on protein-ligand interaction principles and heuristics. To improve interpretability of protein-small molecule binding predictions, we developed the PWRules framework, which applies binding affinity data to identify privileged small molecule fragments and subsequently defines complementary pairing rules between these fragments and protein words (semantic sequence units) through an interpretability module. The resulting word-fragment rules are then ranked by the PWScore function to prioritize active compounds. Evaluations on benchmark datasets show that PWScore achieves competitive performance comparable to the physics-based model (Glide) and the deep learning model (PSICHIC) and shows broad applicability for protein targets outside the training dataset, e.g., SARS-CoV-2 main protease. Notably, PWScore captures complementary interaction information, yielding superior enrichment performance when integrated with these established methods. Structural analysis of protein-ligand complexes indicates that learned word-fragment rules are significantly enriched near ligand-binding pockets, despite training without explicit structural guidance. By extracting and applying complementary pairing rules, PWRules provides an interpretable framework for drug discovery.

</details>


### [89] [Efficient Diffusion Models under Nonconvex Equality and Inequality constraints via Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17838)
*Kijung Jeon,Michael Muehlebach,Molei Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个统一的约束扩散模型框架，用于在非凸可行集上同时处理等式和不等式约束，通过高效的着陆机制替代投影，利用欠阻尼动力学加速混合


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程应用中需要在满足物理、几何或安全约束的集合内进行生成建模（如分子生成、机器人学）。现有方法在处理非凸可行集上的等式和不等式约束时，通常需要昂贵的投影操作，计算成本高且可能失败

Method: 提出统一框架，在扩散过程中同时强制执行等式和不等式约束。关键创新是计算高效的着陆机制，替代传统投影方法。结合过阻尼和欠阻尼动力学进行前向和后向采样，利用欠阻尼动力学加速向先验分布的混合

Result: 在包含等式和混合约束的基准测试中，该方法实现了与最先进基线相当的样本质量，同时显著降低了计算成本。减少了训练和推理期间的功能评估次数和内存使用

Conclusion: 该框架为在非凸可行集上的扩散模型提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案，通过高效的着陆机制和欠阻尼动力学，在保持样本质量的同时大幅降低计算复杂度

Abstract: Generative modeling within constrained sets is essential for scientific and engineering applications involving physical, geometric, or safety requirements (e.g., molecular generation, robotics). We present a unified framework for constrained diffusion models on generic nonconvex feasible sets $Σ$ that simultaneously enforces equality and inequality constraints throughout the diffusion process. Our framework incorporates both overdamped and underdamped dynamics for forward and backward sampling. A key algorithmic innovation is a computationally efficient landing mechanism that replaces costly and often ill-defined projections onto $Σ$, ensuring feasibility without iterative Newton solves or projection failures. By leveraging underdamped dynamics, we accelerate mixing toward the prior distribution, effectively alleviating the high simulation costs typically associated with constrained diffusion. Empirically, this approach reduces function evaluations and memory usage during both training and inference while preserving sample quality. On benchmarks featuring equality and mixed constraints, our method achieves comparable sample quality to state-of-the-art baselines while significantly reducing computational cost, providing a practical and scalable solution for diffusion on nonconvex feasible sets.

</details>


### [90] [LLM as a Tool, Not an Agent: Code-Mined Tree Transformations for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16555)
*Masakazu Yoshimura,Zitang Sun,Yuiko Sakuma,Junji Otsuka,Atsushi Irie,Takeshi Ohashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMasTool：一种基于分层树的神经架构搜索框架，通过结合算法搜索与LLM辅助，实现稳定且开放式的模型演化，超越传统NAS方法和纯LLM方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算法驱动的NAS依赖精心设计的手工搜索空间，限制了开放式探索；而基于LLM的编码方法虽然减少了手动设计，但LLM难以可靠生成复杂有效的架构，且存在训练数据偏差问题。需要结合可靠的算法搜索与强大的LLM辅助

Method: 提出LLMasTool分层树基NAS框架：1）从任意源代码自动提取可重用模块，将完整架构表示为分层树；2）通过可靠的树变换而非代码生成进行演化；3）粗粒度规划由多样性引导算法控制，利用贝叶斯建模提高探索效率；4）LLM解决剩余自由度，确保有意义的演化轨迹和可执行架构

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet16-120数据集上，分别比现有NAS方法提升0.69、1.83和2.68个百分点，证明了方法的有效性

Conclusion: LLMasTool通过分层树表示和算法-LLM协同演化，实现了稳定、开放式的神经架构搜索，克服了纯算法NAS的搜索空间限制和纯LLM方法的可靠性问题，能够探索超越LLM固有偏差的多样化方向

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automatically discover high-performing deep neural network (DNN) architectures. However, conventional algorithm-driven NAS relies on carefully hand-crafted search spaces to ensure executability, which restricts open-ended exploration. Recent coding-based agentic approaches using large language models (LLMs) reduce manual design, but current LLMs struggle to reliably generate complex, valid architectures, and their proposals are often biased toward a narrow set of patterns observed in their training data. To bridge reliable algorithmic search with powerful LLM assistance, we propose LLMasTool, a hierarchical tree-based NAS framework for stable and open-ended model evolution. Our method automatically extracts reusable modules from arbitrary source code and represents full architectures as hierarchical trees, enabling evolution through reliable tree transformations rather than code generation. At each evolution step, coarse-level planning is governed by a diversity-guided algorithm that leverages Bayesian modeling to improve exploration efficiency, while the LLM resolves the remaining degrees of freedom to ensure a meaningful evolutionary trajectory and an executable generated architecture. With this formulation, instead of fully agentic LLM approaches, our method explores diverse directions beyond the inherent biases in the LLM. Our method improves over existing NAS methods by 0.69, 1.83, and 2.68 points on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet16-120, demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [91] [Online Conformal Prediction with Adversarial Semi-bandit Feedback via Regret Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17984)
*Junyoung Yang,Kyungmin Kim,Sangdon Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种针对部分反馈场景的在线保形预测方法，通过将预测集选择建模为对抗性多臂老虎机问题，实现长期覆盖率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线保形预测方法通常假设完全反馈（真实标签总是可观测），但在实际安全关键系统中，真实标签可能只在预测集内时才被揭示，这种部分反馈场景更具挑战性。

Method: 将在线保形预测问题形式化为对抗性多臂老虎机问题，将每个候选预测集视为一个臂。基于现有对抗性老虎机算法，通过建立学习器遗憾与覆盖率之间的明确联系来实现长期覆盖率保证。

Result: 在独立同分布和非独立同分布设置下进行实证验证，表明该方法能成功控制误覆盖率，同时保持合理的预测集大小。

Conclusion: 该方法为部分反馈场景下的在线不确定性量化提供了有效的解决方案，扩展了在线保形预测的应用范围，特别是在真实标签仅当位于预测集内时才可观测的挑战性环境中。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in safety-critical systems, where decisions must be made under uncertainty. In particular, we consider the problem of online uncertainty quantification, where data points arrive sequentially. Online conformal prediction is a principled online uncertainty quantification method that dynamically constructs a prediction set at each time step. While existing methods for online conformal prediction provide long-run coverage guarantees without any distributional assumptions, they typically assume a full feedback setting in which the true label is always observed. In this paper, we propose a novel learning method for online conformal prediction with partial feedback from an adaptive adversary-a more challenging setup where the true label is revealed only when it lies inside the constructed prediction set. Specifically, we formulate online conformal prediction as an adversarial bandit problem by treating each candidate prediction set as an arm. Building on an existing algorithm for adversarial bandits, our method achieves a long-run coverage guarantee by explicitly establishing its connection to the regret of the learner. Finally, we empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in both independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) and non-i.i.d. settings, showing that it successfully controls the miscoverage rate while maintaining a reasonable size of the prediction set.

</details>


### [92] [S-GRPO: Unified Post-Training for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16557)
*Yuming Yan,Kai Tang,Sihong Chen,Ke Xu,Dan Hu,Qun Yu,Pengfei Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出S-GRPO框架，通过条件性真实轨迹注入机制，将监督学习与强化学习优势结合，解决视觉任务后训练中SFT的灾难性遗忘和RL的优化崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型的后训练方法存在局限性：监督微调（SFT）强制模型沿单一专家轨迹生成，导致分布偏移引发灾难性遗忘；强化学习（RL）探索多个轨迹但常遭遇优化崩溃问题，即未对齐模型在稀疏奖励视觉任务中无法自发采样有效轨迹。需要统一框架来整合两者的优势。

Method: 提出监督组相对策略优化（S-GRPO）框架，核心是条件性真实轨迹注入（CGI）机制。当二元验证器检测到采样轨迹组完全探索失败时，CGI将已验证的真实轨迹注入候选池，并赋予确定性最大奖励。这使真实轨迹成为组相对优势估计中的高优势锚点，将监督学习目标重新表述为策略梯度的高优势分量，实现专家轨迹利用与新颖视觉概念探索的动态平衡。

Result: 理论分析和实证结果表明，S-GRPO优雅地弥合了SFT与RL之间的差距，大幅加速收敛速度，在实现优越领域适应的同时保留了基础模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: S-GRPO通过创新的条件性真实轨迹注入机制，为视觉任务后训练提供了一个统一框架，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，实现了监督学习与强化学习的协同优化。

Abstract: Current post-training methodologies for adapting Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) generally fall into two paradigms: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL). Despite their prevalence, both approaches suffer from inefficiencies when applied in isolation. SFT forces the model's generation along a single expert trajectory, often inducing catastrophic forgetting of general multimodal capabilities due to distributional shifts. Conversely, RL explores multiple generated trajectories but frequently encounters optimization collapse - a cold-start problem where an unaligned model fails to spontaneously sample any domain-valid trajectories in sparse-reward visual tasks. In this paper, we propose Supervised Group Relative Policy Optimization (S-GRPO), a unified post-training framework that integrates the guidance of imitation learning into the multi-trajectory exploration of preference optimization. Tailored for direct-generation visual tasks, S-GRPO introduces Conditional Ground-Truth Trajectory Injection (CGI). When a binary verifier detects a complete exploratory failure within a sampled group of trajectories, CGI injects the verified ground-truth trajectory into the candidate pool. By assigning a deterministic maximal reward to this injected anchor, S-GRPO enforces a positive signal within the group-relative advantage estimation. This mechanism reformulates the supervised learning objective as a high-advantage component of the policy gradient, compelling the model to dynamically balance between exploiting the expert trajectory and exploring novel visual concepts. Theoretical analysis and empirical results demonstrate that S-GRPO gracefully bridges the gap between SFT and RL, drastically accelerates convergence, and achieves superior domain adaptation while preserving the base model's general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [93] [Towards E-Value Based Stopping Rules for Bayesian Deep Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18089)
*Emanuel Sommer,Rickmer Schulte,Sarah Deubner,Julius Kobialka,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于E值的停止规则，用于贝叶斯深度集成中MCMC采样的早期停止，以决定何时停止采样过程


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯深度集成结合了深度集成的鲁棒性和MCMC的灵活性，但MCMC采样成本高昂。在优化深度集成后添加采样能显著改进性能，但需要确定何时停止采样以获得显著改进

Method: 提出基于E值的停止规则，将集成构建表述为顺序任意时间有效假设检验，用于拒绝"MCMC不提供改进"的零假设，实现早期停止采样

Result: 实证研究表明该方法有效，通常只需要完整链预算的一小部分就能获得显著改进

Conclusion: 基于E值的停止规则为贝叶斯深度集成中的MCMC采样提供了原则性的早期停止方法，能有效平衡计算成本和性能改进

Abstract: Bayesian Deep Ensembles (BDEs) represent a powerful approach for uncertainty quantification in deep learning, combining the robustness of Deep Ensembles (DEs) with flexible multi-chain MCMC. While DEs are affordable in most deep learning settings, (long) sampling of Bayesian neural networks can be prohibitively costly. Yet, adding sampling after optimizing the DEs has been shown to yield significant improvements. This leaves a critical practical question: How long should the sequential sampling process continue to yield significant improvements over the initial optimized DE baseline? To tackle this question, we propose a stopping rule based on E-values. We formulate the ensemble construction as a sequential anytime-valid hypothesis test, providing a principled way to decide whether or not to reject the null hypothesis that MCMC offers no improvement over a strong baseline, to early stop the sampling. Empirically, we study this approach for diverse settings. Our results demonstrate the efficacy of our approach and reveal that only a fraction of the full-chain budget is often required.

</details>


### [94] [Cross-Modal Generation: From Commodity WiFi to High-Fidelity mmWave and RFID Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16558)
*Zhixiong Yang,Long Jing,Yao Li,Shuli Cheng,Guoxuan Chi,Chenyu Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RF-CMG：一种基于扩散的跨模态生成方法，利用数据丰富的WiFi信号合成稀缺模态（mmWave和RFID）的高保真RF数据，通过解耦高频引导和低频约束解决无线领域数据不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线领域存在显著的数据不平衡问题：WiFi数据丰富，但mmWave和RFID数据稀缺（采集成本高），这导致难以训练高质量生成模型。需要利用数据丰富的模态来辅助生成数据稀缺的模态。

Method: 提出RF-CMG方法，核心是将跨模态生成解耦为高频引导和低频约束：1）高频引导从有限目标模态数据中学习高频分布；2）低频约束在生成过程中保持底层物理结构。具体包括：模态引导嵌入（MGE）模块引导反向扩散轨迹朝向目标高频分布；低频模态一致性（LFMC）模块逐步强制执行低频约束以抑制源模态结构偏差累积。

Result: 与多种主流生成模型相比，RF-CMG在合成RFID和mmWave信号方面表现出优越性能。生成的合成数据在手势识别任务中有效，并分析了合成数据比例对下游性能的影响。

Conclusion: RF-CMG成功解决了无线领域跨模态数据不平衡问题，通过解耦高频引导和低频约束的机制，能够利用数据丰富的WiFi信号合成高质量的目标模态数据，为数据稀缺的无线模态提供了有效的生成解决方案。

Abstract: AIGC has shown remarkable success in CV and NLP, and has recently demonstrated promising potential in the wireless domain. However, significant data imbalance exists across RF modalities, with abundant WiFi data but scarce mmWave and RFID data due to high acquisition cost. This makes it difficult to train high-quality generative models for these data-scarce modalities. In this work, we propose RF-CMG, a diffusion-based cross-modal generative method that leverages data-rich WiFi signals to synthesize high-fidelity RF data for scarce modalities including mmWave and RFID. The key insight of RF-CMG is to decouple cross-modal generation into high-frequency guidance and low-frequency constraint, which respectively learn high-frequency distribution from limited target modality data and preserve the underlying physical structure via low-frequency constraints during generation. On this basis, we introduce a Modality-Guided Embedding (MGE) module to steer the reverse diffusion trajectory toward the target high-frequency distribution, and a Low-Frequency Modality Consistency (LFMC) module to progressively enforce low-frequency constraints to suppress the accumulation of source-modality structural biases during inference, enabling high-quality target-modality generation. Performance comparison with several prevalent generative models demonstrates that RF-CMG achieves superior performance in synthesizing RFID and mmWave signals. We further showcase the effectiveness of the data generated by RF-CMG in gesture recognition tasks, and analyze the impact of the proportion of synthetic data on downstream performance.

</details>


### [95] [Knowing When to Quit: A Principled Framework for Dynamic Abstention in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18419)
*Hen Davidov,Nachshon Cohen,Oren Kalinsky,Yaron Fairstein,Guy Kushilevitz,Ram Yazdi,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于强化学习的动态中止方法，用于在LLM生成过程中提前终止不正确的推理轨迹，以节省计算资源并提高准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM使用思维链推理时经常产生冗长但错误的响应，浪费大量计算资源。虽然中止方法可以在生成前后决定是否输出，但动态生成过程中止（在生成过程中提前终止无望的推理轨迹）缺乏理论指导。

Method: 将动态中止建模为强化学习框架中的显式动作，通过中止奖励参数控制计算与信息的权衡。提出当中止价值函数低于该奖励时中止生成，并推导了高效近似价值函数的方法。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法在一般条件下严格优于基线方法。在数学推理和毒性避免任务上的实证结果支持理论，并显示出比现有方法更好的选择性准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM的动态中止提供了理论框架和实用方法，能够在节省计算资源的同时提高输出质量，为选择性生成提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) using chain-of-thought reasoning often waste substantial compute by producing long, incorrect responses. Abstention can mitigate this by withholding outputs unlikely to be correct. While most abstention methods decide to withhold outputs before or after generation, dynamic mid-generation abstention considers early termination of unpromising reasoning traces at each token position. Prior work has explored empirical variants of this idea, but principled guidance for the abstention rule remains lacking. We present a formal analysis of dynamic abstention for LLMs, modeling abstention as an explicit action within a regularized reinforcement learning framework. An abstention reward parameter controls the trade-off between compute and information. We show that abstaining when the value function falls below this reward strictly outperforms natural baselines under general conditions. We further derive a principled and efficient method to approximate the value function. Empirical results on mathematical reasoning and toxicity avoidance tasks support our theory and demonstrate improved selective accuracy over existing methods.

</details>


### [96] [Reasoning on the Manifold: Bidirectional Consistency for Self-Verification in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16565)
*Jiaoyang Ruan,Xin Gao,Yinda Chen,Hengyu Zeng,Liang Du,Guanghao Li,Jie Fu,Jian Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BMC（双向流形一致性）指标，通过几何视角评估扩散大语言模型推理轨迹的稳定性，无需训练即可诊断推理有效性、优化推理过程并指导模型对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）在全局规划方面具有结构优势，但如何有效验证其通过有效推理轨迹得出正确答案仍是一个关键挑战。现有方法缺乏对推理过程内在几何稳定性的量化评估。

Method: 提出"流形上的推理"几何视角，假设有效生成轨迹位于学习分布的高密度流形上作为稳定吸引子，而无效路径则表现出离流形漂移。引入BMC（双向流形一致性）指标，通过前向掩码和后向重建循环量化生成序列的稳定性，无需训练且无监督。

Result: BMC在完整推理生命周期中展现出多功能性：1）诊断阶段：作为无需真实答案的解决方案有效性判别器；2）推理阶段：通过拒绝重采样有效集中计算资源于复杂推理任务；3）对齐阶段：作为密集几何奖励，将稀疏结果监督转化为细粒度指导，使模型超越标准基线自我进化。

Conclusion: 内在几何稳定性是dLLMs正确性的可靠指标，BMC提供了一种训练自由的几何评估框架，为扩散语言模型的推理验证、优化和对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: While Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) offer structural advantages for global planning, efficiently verifying that they arrive at correct answers via valid reasoning traces remains a critical challenge. In this work, we propose a geometric perspective: Reasoning on the Manifold. We hypothesize that valid generation trajectories reside as stable attractors on the high-density manifold of the learned distribution, whereas invalid paths exhibit off-manifold drift. To operationalize this, we introduce Bidirectional Manifold Consistency (BMC), a training-free, unsupervised metric that quantifies the stability of the generated sequence through a forward-masking and backward-reconstruction cycle. Empirically, we demonstrate BMC's versatility across the full reasoning lifecycle: (1) in Diagnosis, it serves as a robust discriminator of solution validity without ground truth answer; (2) in Inference, it enables rejection resampling to effectively concentrate computational resources on complex reasoning tasks; and (3) in Alignment, it functions as a dense geometric reward that transforms sparse outcome supervision into fine-grained guidance, empowering models to self-evolve beyond standard baselines. Our results establish intrinsic geometric stability as a robust indicator of correctness for dLLMs.

</details>


### [97] [In Search of Lost DNA Sequence Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16570)
*Zhijiang Tang,Jiaxin Qi,Yan Cui,Jinli Ou,Yuhua Zheng,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出DNA序列预训练研究中存在三个被忽视的关键问题：不合适的下游数据集、邻近掩码策略的内在缺陷、以及词汇表讨论的缺乏，并提出了相应的指导原则和标准化测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模DNA序列预训练取得了显著进展，但现有研究过度强调预训练规模和自定义下游评估数据集，而忽视了预训练范式中一些基本组成部分。作者发现DNA预训练中存在三个关键但被忽视的问题。

Method: 通过全面调查提出原则性指导方针，包括评估数据集的选择标准、任务设计指导以及深入的词汇表分析。进行大量实验验证所发现问题的重要性，并引入标准化测试平台以实现DNA预训练方法的可重复和严格基准测试。

Result: 大量实验验证了所识别问题的重要性，并支持了建议的合理性。标准化测试平台能够促进基因组基础模型的开发。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了DNA预训练中的关键问题，提出了系统性的解决方案和指导原则，并通过标准化测试平台为基因组基础模型的发展提供了可重复的基准测试框架。

Abstract: DNA sequence encoding is fundamental to gene function prediction, protein synthesis, and diverse downstream biological tasks. Despite the substantial progress achieved by large-scale DNA sequence pretraining, existing studies have overwhelmingly emphasized pretraining scale and custom downstream evaluation datasets, while neglecting some essential components of the pretraining paradigm. In this paper, we reveal three critical yet heretofore overlooked problems in DNA pretraining: inappropriate downstream datasets, inherent flaws in the neighbor-masking strategy, and the lack of detailed discussion on vocabulary. Therefore, we undertake comprehensive investigations and propose principled guidelines, including selection criteria for evaluation datasets, guiding task design, and in-depth vocabulary analysis. Extensive experiments validate the significance of our identified problems and support the rationale behind our recommendations. Finally, we introduce a standardized testbed that enables reproducible and rigorous benchmarking of DNA pretraining methods to advance the development of genomic foundation models.

</details>


### [98] [FedOBP: Federated Optimal Brain Personalization through Cloud-Edge Element-wise Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16574)
*Xingyan Chen,Tian Du,Changqiao Xu,Fuzhen Zhuang,Lujie Zhong,Gabriel-Miro Muntean,Enmao Diao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedOBP算法通过量化阈值机制和元素级重要性评分，将经典剪枝理论与联邦参数解耦相结合，在服务器端计算参数重要性，实现高效个性化联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临客户端数据异构和资源受限移动设备的挑战，个性化联邦学习通过将共享全局知识适配到本地数据分布来解决此问题。模型解耦方法将模型分为全局和个性化参数，但关键问题在于如何选择应个性化的参数以平衡全局知识共享和本地适配。

Method: 提出FedOBP算法，包含量化阈值机制和元素级重要性评分。该评分扩展了OBD剪枝理论，通过纳入泰勒展开中的一阶导数联邦近似来评估每个参数对个性化的重要性。将原本在客户端执行的度量计算移至服务器端，减轻资源受限移动设备的负担。

Result: 在多种数据集和异构场景下的广泛实验表明，FedOBP优于最先进方法，同时仅需个性化极少量的参数。

Conclusion: 这是首个将经典基于显著性的剪枝理论与联邦参数解耦相结合的工作，为基于参数对本地损失景观敏感性的个性化参数选择提供了严格的理论依据。FedOBP在保持高性能的同时显著减少了需要个性化的参数数量。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) faces challenges from client data heterogeneity and resource-constrained mobile devices, which can degrade model accuracy. Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) addresses this issue by adapting shared global knowledge to local data distributions. A promising approach in PFL is model decoupling, which separates the model into global and personalized parameters, raising the key question of which parameters should be personalized to balance global knowledge sharing and local adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Federated Optimal Brain Personalization (FedOBP) algorithm with a quantile-based thresholding mechanism and introduce an element-wise importance score. This score extends Optimal Brain Damage (OBD) pruning theory by incorporating a federated approximation of the first-order derivative in the Taylor expansion to evaluate the importance of each parameter for personalization. Moreover, we move the metric computation originally performed on clients to the server side, to alleviate the burden on resource-constrained mobile devices. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to bridge classical saliency-based pruning theory with federated parameter decoupling, providing a rigorous theoretical justification for selecting personalized parameters based on their sensitivity to local loss landscapes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedOBP outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse datasets and heterogeneity scenarios, while requiring personalization of only a very small number of personalized parameters.

</details>


### [99] [Towards Trustworthy Depression Estimation via Disentangled Evidential Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16579)
*Fangyuan Liu,Sirui Zhao,Zeyu Zhang,Jinyang Huang,Feng-Qi Cui,Bin Luo,Tong Xu,Meng Li,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EviDep：基于证据学习的抑郁症评估框架，通过Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布联合量化抑郁严重程度及不确定性，采用频率感知特征提取和解耦证据学习策略解决多模态融合中的冗余问题，实现鲁棒可信的临床筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有确定性方法在真实世界部署中对信号损坏和环境噪声高度脆弱，产生未校准的点估计，使安全关键的临床系统面临过度自信误诊的严重风险。多模态证据融合中存在跨模态冗余积累的结构性缺陷，会人为夸大诊断置信度。

Method: 提出EviDep证据学习框架：1) 频率感知特征提取模块：基于小波的混合专家系统动态隔离任务无关噪声；2) 解耦证据学习策略：分离共享共识与模态特定细微差异；3) 通过Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布联合量化抑郁严重程度、偶然不确定性和认知不确定性；4) 在贝叶斯融合前显式解耦表示以系统缓解证据冗余。

Result: 在AVEC 2013、2014、DAIC-WOZ和E-DAIC数据集上的广泛实验证实，EviDep实现了最先进的预测精度和优越的不确定性校准，为可信临床筛查提供了鲁棒的故障安全机制。

Conclusion: EviDep建立了高度弹性和可信的评估范式，通过严格的信息完整性保证鲁棒证据合成，解决了多模态证据融合中的冗余积累问题，为安全关键的抑郁症自动评估提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Automated depression estimation is highly vulnerable to signal corruption and ambient noise in real-world deployment. Prevailing deterministic methods produce uncalibrated point estimates, exposing safety-critical clinical systems to the severe risk of overconfident misdiagnoses. To establish a highly resilient and trustworthy assessment paradigm, we propose EviDep, an evidential learning framework that jointly quantifies depression severity alongside aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties via a Normal-Inverse-Gamma distribution. A fundamental vulnerability in multimodal evidential fusion is the uncontrolled accumulation of cross-modal redundancies. This structural flaw artificially inflates diagnostic confidence by double-counting overlapping evidence. To guarantee robust evidence synthesis, EviDep enforces strict information integrity. First, a Frequency-aware Feature Extraction module leverages a wavelet-based Mixture-of-Experts to dynamically isolate task-irrelevant noise, preserving the fidelity of diagnostic signals. Subsequently, a Disentangled Evidential Learning strategy separates the shared consensus from modality-specific nuances. By explicitly decoupling these representations before Bayesian fusion, EviDep systematically mitigates evidence redundancy. Extensive experiments on AVEC 2013, 2014, DAIC-WOZ, and E-DAIC confirm that EviDep achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy and superior uncertainty calibration, delivering a robust fail-safe mechanism for trustworthy clinical screening.

</details>


### [100] [Continuous ageing trajectory representations for knee-aware lifetime prediction of lithium-ion batteries across heterogeneous dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16580)
*Agnieszka Pregowska,Stefan Marynowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于连续表示的电池老化分析统一框架，从异构公开数据集中学习电压-容量和容量-循环轨迹，实现退化描述符一致提取和早期寿命预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂离子电池老化评估面临电池间变异性、异构循环协议和数据驱动模型跨数据集可迁移性有限等挑战，特别是退化转变点（如拐点）的鲁棒识别和剩余使用寿命的可靠早期预测仍是未解决问题

Method: 基于连续表示的电池老化分析框架，从NASA、CALCE、ISU-ILCC等异构公开数据集中学习电压-容量和容量-循环轨迹的连续表示，实现退化描述符（曲率、平台长度、拐点相关指标）的一致提取，减少对数据集特定离散化的敏感性

Result: 在250多个电池上观察到拐点起始与寿命终止之间的统计显著相关性（Pearson 0.75-0.84）；早期寿命分析确认拐点相关特征在部分轨迹估计时仍具预测价值；早期寿命模型随观测循环数增加提供越来越稳定的RUL预测，前5-20个循环内出现有意义的预测性能，且在跨数据集域偏移下保持鲁棒

Conclusion: 该框架整合连续建模、特征提取和不确定性感知预测，提供可解释且数据集一致的方法，在异构数据集类型间展现鲁棒性；相比传统离散或基于特征的方法，该表示减少了对采样分辨率的敏感性并提高了跨数据集一致性；研究局限于实验室规模数据集和基于容量的寿命终止定义

Abstract: Accurate assessment of lithium-ion battery ageing is challenged by cell-to-cell variability, heterogeneous cycling protocols, and limited transferability of data-driven models across datasets. In particular, robust identification of degradation transitions, such as the knee point, and reliable early-life prediction of remaining useful life (RUL) remain open problems. This study proposes a unified framework for battery ageing analysis based on continuous representations of voltage-capacity and capacity-cycle trajectories learned from heterogeneous public datasets (NASA, CALCE, ISU-ILCC). The continuous formulation enables consistent extraction of degradation descriptors, including curvature, plateau length and knee-related metrics, while reducing sensitivity to dataset-specific discretisation. Across more than 250 cells, statistically significant correlations between knee onset and end-of-life (Pearson 0.75-0.84) are observed. Additional early-life analysis confirms that knee-related features retain predictive value when estimated from partial trajectories. Early-life models provide increasingly stable RUL predictions as the number of observed cycles increases, with meaningful predictive performance emerging within the first 5-20 cycles and remain robust under cross-dataset domain shift. The framework integrates continuous modelling, feature extraction and uncertainty-aware prediction, providing an interpretable and dataset-consistent approach demonstrating robustness across heterogeneous dataset types. Compared with conventional discrete or feature-based methods, the proposed representation reduces sensitivity to sampling resolution and improves cross-dataset consistency. The study is limited to laboratory-scale datasets and capacity-based end-of-life definitions.

</details>


### [101] [NCO4CVRP: Neural Combinatorial Optimization for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16581)
*Mahir Labib Dihan,Md. Ashrafur Rahman Khan,Wasif Jalal,Md. Roqunuzzaman Sojib,Mashroor Hasan Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过改进神经组合优化(NCO)的推理技术，将模拟退火集成到RRC方法中，并在POMO中引入波束搜索，显著提升了CVRP问题的求解质量和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化框架的推理技术存在局限性，容易陷入局部最优，且缺乏系统性的搜索策略，限制了解决方案的质量和模型泛化能力，需要改进推理方法来提升实际应用效果。

Method: 1. 改进LEHD模型的RRC方法：集成模拟退火(SA)，引入概率接受机制替代贪婪替换，帮助模型逃离局部最优；2. 增强POMO方法：集成波束搜索，系统探索多个有前景的解决方案；3. 研究多种推理策略：包括Softmax采样、贪婪、Gumbel-Softmax和Epsilon-Greedy；4. 探索实例增强技术：水平和垂直翻转、基于旋转的增强，提升模型泛化能力。

Result: 实验表明，改进方法在各种CVRP基准测试中显著降低了最优性差距，波束搜索和基于SA的RRC方法始终表现出优越性能，提升了解决方案质量和模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过改进推理技术和利用增强的搜索策略，该工作提升了神经组合优化模型在现实世界组合优化任务中的适用性，为NCO框架的实际应用提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) has emerged as a powerful framework for solving combinatorial optimization problems by integrating deep learning-based models. This work focuses on improving existing inference techniques to enhance solution quality and generalization. Specifically, we modify the Random Re-Construct (RRC) approach of the Light Encoder Heavy Decoder (LEHD) model by incorporating Simulated Annealing (SA). Unlike the conventional RRC, which greedily replaces suboptimal segments, our SA-based modification introduces a probabilistic acceptance mechanism that allows the model to escape local optima and explore a more diverse solution space. Additionally, we enhance the Policy Optimization with Multiple Optima (POMO) approach by integrating Beam Search, enabling systematic exploration of multiple promising solutions while maintaining diversity in the search space. We further investigate different inference strategies, including Softmax Sampling, Greedy, Gumbel-Softmax, and Epsilon-Greedy, analyzing their impact on solution quality. Furthermore, we explore instance augmentation techniques, such as horizontal and vertical flipping and rotation-based augmentations, to improve model generalization across different CVRP instances. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that these modifications significantly reduce the optimality gap across various Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) benchmarks, with Beam Search and SA-based RRC consistently yielding superior performance. By refining inference techniques and leveraging enhanced search strategies, our work contributes to the broader applicability of NCO models in real-world combinatorial optimization tasks.

</details>


### [102] [POLAR: Online Learning for LoRA Adapter Caching and Routing in Edge LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16583)
*Shaoang Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出POLAR框架，解决边缘部署LLM时LoRA适配器的缓存与路由联合优化问题，通过两时间尺度的上下文老虎机方法，在保证路由学习效率的同时管理内存层次结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署大型语言模型时，GPU/DRAM只能容纳少量LoRA适配器，服务请求需要从存储中分页加载非驻留适配器权重，造成显著延迟。这形成了缓存决策（慢时间尺度）和路由决策（快时间尺度）的耦合优化问题。

Method: 提出POLAR框架：1) 缓存感知的LinUCB路由器处理快时间尺度的请求路由；2) 基于周期的缓存控制器管理慢时间尺度的适配器驻留。设计了固定周期版本和周期倍增版本(POLAR+)，后者加入强制探索和优化的缓存管理。

Result: 理论分析显示：固定周期版本在任意上下文下具有最坏情况遗憾保证；POLAR+在随机规律性和缓存性条件下达到$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{NT}+\sqrt{KT})$的亚线性遗憾。实验使用15个真实LoRA适配器和实测GPU分页延迟，显示自适应缓存控制显著优于非自适应基线。

Conclusion: POLAR框架有效解决了边缘LLM部署中的适配器缓存与路由联合优化问题，证明内存层次结构不会从根本上减慢路由学习，自适应缓存控制能显著提升系统性能。

Abstract: Edge deployment of large language models (LLMs) increasingly relies on libraries of lightweight LoRA adapters, yet GPU/DRAM can keep only a small resident subset at a time. Serving a request through a non-resident adapter requires paging its weights from storage, incurring measurable latency. This creates a two-timescale online control problem: on a slow timescale, the system selects which adapters remain resident in fast memory, while on a fast timescale it routes each request to an adapter whose context-dependent utility is unknown a priori. The two decisions are tightly coupled: the cache determines the cost of exploration, and the router determines which adapters receive informative feedback. We formulate this joint caching-and-routing problem as a two-timescale contextual bandit and propose POLAR (Paging and Online Learning for Adapter Routing). POLAR pairs a cache-aware LinUCB router with an epoch-based cache controller. We study two variants. A fixed-epoch version provides a robust baseline with worst-case regret guarantees under arbitrary contexts. An epoch-doubling version, POLAR+, adds forced exploration and improved cache optimization to achieve $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{NT}+\sqrt{KT})$ sublinear regret under stochastic regularity and cacheability conditions, where $N$ is the adapter count, $K$ the cache size, $d$ the context dimension, and $T$ the horizon. The routing term matches the standard contextual-bandit rate up to logarithmic factors, showing that the memory hierarchy does not fundamentally slow routing learning. Experiments using 15 real LoRA adapters for Qwen2.5-7B together with measured GPU paging latencies show that adaptive cache control substantially outperforms non-adaptive baselines and exhibits scaling trends consistent with the theory.

</details>


### [103] [A Systematic Survey and Benchmark of Deep Learning for Molecular Property Prediction in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16586)
*Zongru Li,Xingsheng Chen,Honggang Wen,Regina Qianru Zhang,Ming Li,Xiaojin Zhang,Hongzhi Yin,Qiang Yang,Kwok-Yan Lam,Pietro Lio,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 分子性质预测综述：系统梳理量子化学、化学信息学与深度学习的融合，提出统一分类法，分析现有基准的局限性，并展望物理感知学习、不确定性校准基础模型和多模态基准生态系统三大发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测是连接分子结构与理化生物行为的关键桥梁，但现有研究缺乏系统性的分类框架，且基准数据集存在数据质量、分割策略和评估协议等方面的挑战，需要现代化改进以支持更可靠的研究。

Method: 提出统一分类法，涵盖四种互补范式：量子方法、描述符机器学习、几何深度学习和基础模型。通过整合广泛使用的数据集和反映工业视角的数据集进行基准分析，涵盖量子、理化、生理和生物物理领域。

Result: 识别出当前基准设计中的关键问题：不一致的立体化学、异质测定来源、随机或定义不清的分割导致的再现性限制。这些发现促使基准设计向更透明、时间感知和骨架感知的方法现代化。

Conclusion: 提出三个前瞻性方向：1)嵌入量子一致性的物理感知学习；2)用于可信推理的不确定性校准基础模型；3)整合计算与实验数据的现实多模态基准生态系统。这些方向将推动分子性质预测向更可靠、可解释和实用的方向发展。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction integrates quantum chemistry, cheminformatics, and deep learning to connect molecular structure with physicochemical and biological behavior. This survey traces four complementary paradigms, including Quantum, Descriptor Machine Learning, Geometric Deep Learning, and Foundation Models, and outlines a unified taxonomy linking molecular representations, model architectures, and interdisciplinary applications. Benchmark analyses integrate evidence from both widely used datasets and datasets reflecting industry perspectives, encompassing quantum, physicochemical, physiological, and biophysical domains. The survey examines current standards in data curation, splitting strategies, and evaluation protocols, highlighting challenges including inconsistent stereochemistry, heterogeneous assay sources, and reproducibility limitations under random or poorly defined splits. These observations motivate the modernization of benchmark design toward more transparent, time- and scaffold-aware methodologies. We further propose three forward-looking directions: (i) physics-aware learning embedding quantum consistency, (ii) uncertainty-calibrated foundation models for trustworthy inference, and (iii) realistic multimodal benchmark ecosystems integrating computational and experimental data. Repository: https://github.com/Zongru-Li/Survey-and-Benchmarks-of-DL-for-Molecular-Property-Prediction-in-the-Foundation-Model-Era.

</details>


### [104] [Hybrid Spectro-Temporal Fusion Framework for Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16589)
*Jongyeop Kim,Jinki Kim,Doyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出结合到达时间间隔描述符与频谱特征的谱时对齐和混合谱时融合框架，用于振动结构健康监测，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测对确保结构安全至关重要，需要分析工程系统的振动响应。传统方法可能无法充分捕捉振动动力学的多尺度特征，需要更全面的表征方法。

Method: 提出谱时对齐框架和混合谱时融合框架，整合到达时间间隔描述符与频谱特征，捕捉精细尺度和粗尺度振动动力学。通过LDS V406电动振动台收集数据进行实验验证。

Result: 提出的谱时表征显著优于传统输入形式。时间分辨率Δτ=0.02适合传统机器学习模型，而Δτ=0.008能充分发挥深度学习架构性能。混合框架在准确性和稳定性方面均优于基线和仅对齐方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于振动的结构健康监测提供了稳健、准确和可靠的解决方案，通过综合谱时特征表征实现了更好的性能表现。

Abstract: Structural health monitoring plays a critical role in ensuring structural safety by analyzing vibration responses from engineering systems. This paper proposes a Spectro-Temporal Alignment framework and a Hybrid Spectro-Temporal Fusion framework that integrate arrival-time interval descriptors with spectral features to capture both fine-scale and coarse-scale vibration dynamics. Experiments conducted on data collected from an LDS V406 electrodynamic shaker demonstrate that the proposed spectro-temporal representations significantly outperform conventional input formulations. The results indicate that a temporal resolution (Δτ) of 0.008 of 0.02 favors traditional machine learning models, whereas a finer resolution (Δτ) of 0.008 effectively unlocks the performance potential of deep learning architectures. Beyond classification accuracy, a comprehensive stability analysis based on condensed indices, including mean performance, standard deviation, coefficient of variation, and balanced score, shows that the proposed hybrid framework consistently achieves higher accuracy with substantially lower variability compared to baseline and alignment-only approaches. Overall, these results demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a robust, accurate, and reliable solution for vibration-based structural health monitoring.

</details>


### [105] [Global Attention with Linear Complexity for Exascale Generative Data Assimilation in Earth System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16590)
*Xiao Wang,Zezhong Zhang,Isaac Lyngaas,Hong-Jun Yoon,Jong-Youl Choi,Siming Liang,Janet Wang,Hristo G. Chipilski,Ashwin M. Aji,Feng Bao,Peter Jan van Leeuwen,Dan Lu,Guannan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一的单阶段生成式数据同化框架STORM，通过时空Transformer和线性复杂度全局注意力算法，在Frontier超计算机上实现高效的大规模地球系统状态推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管百亿亿次计算显著推进了地球模拟，但可扩展且准确的地球系统状态推断仍然是基本瓶颈，限制了不确定性量化和极端事件预测。

Method: 引入统一的单阶段生成式数据同化框架，将同化重新表述为贝叶斯后验采样，用计算密集、GPU高效的推断替代传统的预报-更新循环。核心是STORM——一种具有全局注意力线性复杂度缩放算法的新型时空Transformer，突破了二次注意力屏障。

Result: 在Frontier超级计算机的32,768个GPU上，该方法实现了63%的强扩展效率和1.6 ExaFLOP的持续性能。进一步扩展到200亿个时空token，实现了超过177k时间帧的公里尺度全球建模，达到了先前无法达到的规模。

Conclusion: 该方法为地球系统预测建立了新范式，能够处理先前无法达到的大规模时空建模问题，显著提升了数据同化的可扩展性和计算效率。

Abstract: Accurate weather and climate prediction relies on data assimilation (DA), which estimates the Earth system state by integrating observations with models. While exascale computing has significantly advanced earth simulation, scalable and accurate inference of the Earth system state remains a fundamental bottleneck, limiting uncertainty quantification and prediction of extreme events. We introduce a unified one-stage generative DA framework that reformulates assimilation as Bayesian posterior sampling, replacing the conventional forecast-update cycle with compute-dense, GPU-efficient inference. At the core is STORM, a novel spatiotemporal transformer with a global attention linear-complexity scaling algorithm that breaks the quadratic attention barrier. On 32,768 GPUs of the Frontier supercomputer, our method achieves 63% strong scaling efficiency and 1.6 ExaFLOP sustained performance. We further scale to 20 billion spatiotemporal tokens, enabling km-scale global modeling over 177k temporal frames, regimes previously unreachable, establishing a new paradigm for Earth system prediction.

</details>


### [106] [Randomized Antipodal Search Done Right for Data Pareto Improvement of LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16591)
*Ziwen Liu,Huawei Lin,Yide Ran,Denghui Zhang,Jianwen Xie,Chuan Li,Weijie Zhao,Zhaozhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RASLIK方法，通过随机化反极搜索和线性化影响核，解决LLM遗忘学习中数据检索的核心挑战，实现遗忘与保留的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习遗忘方法主要关注参数优化，假设遗忘集和保留集容易获取，但实际中遗忘通常由推理时的不良生成触发，数据检索成为核心挑战。需要解决如何有效检索相关数据以支持遗忘学习的问题。

Method: 提出数据帕累托改进概念，并设计RASLIK算法：结合置换投影哈希与随机化反极搜索，在线性化影响核上进行操作，降低选择方差，实现亚线性复杂度。

Result: RASLIK在多个模型、数据集和遗忘算法中，一致优于确定性基线方法甚至oracle采样，在质量和效率上均获得双重增益，验证了随机化搜索作为数据中心遗忘学习的原理性和可扩展解决方案。

Conclusion: 随机化搜索是解决LLM遗忘学习中数据检索挑战的有效方法，RASLIK通过数据帕累托改进原则，为实际部署中的遗忘需求提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) sometimes memorize undesirable knowledge, which must be removed after deployment. Prior work on machine unlearning has focused largely on optimization methods that adjust parameters to enforce forgetting while preserving retention. However, these approaches assume that the forget and retain sets are readily available, which rarely holds in practice. Unlearning is typically triggered by an undesired generation at inference time, making the retrieval of relevant data the central challenge.
  We introduce the notion of data Pareto improvement for LLM unlearning, which formalizes how retrieval can expand the achievable trade-off frontier between forgetting and retention. To realize this principle, we propose Randomized Antipodal Search on Linearized Influence Kernel (RASLIK), a retrieval algorithm that combines permutation-projection hashing with randomized antipodal search. RASLIK reduces selection variance, achieves sublinear complexity, and yields a double gain in both quality and efficiency. Across multiple models, datasets, and unlearning algorithms, RASLIK consistently outperforms deterministic baselines and even oracle sampling, establishing randomized search as a principled and scalable solution for data-centric unlearning.

</details>


### [107] [FedLLM: A Privacy-Preserving Federated Large Language Model for Explainable Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16612)
*Seerat Kaur,Sukhjit Singh Sehra,Dariush Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedLLM框架，结合联邦学习和领域适应的大语言模型，实现隐私保护、可解释的多时间尺度交通流预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测方法存在集中式训练、依赖数值表示、可解释性有限等问题，且大语言模型方法通常假设集中数据可用性，未能充分捕捉真实交通系统的分布式和异构特性

Method: 提出FedLLM框架，包含四个关键贡献：1) 数据驱动的自由选择复合选择分数；2) 在结构化交通提示上微调的领域适应LLM；3) 联邦学习框架，仅交换轻量级LoRA适配器参数；4) 支持上下文推理和跨区域泛化的结构化提示表示

Result: FedLLM在预测性能上优于集中式基线方法，同时产生结构化和可解释的输出，支持非独立同分布交通分布下的学习

Conclusion: 结合联邦学习与领域适应LLM的方法具有实现可扩展、隐私感知和可解释交通预测的潜力

Abstract: Traffic prediction plays a central role in intelligent transportation systems (ITS) by supporting real-time decision-making, congestion management, and long-term planning. However, many existing approaches face practical limitations. Most spatio-temporal models are trained on centralized data, rely on numerical representations, and offer limited explainability. Recent Large Language Model (LLM) methods improve reasoning capabilities but typically assume centralized data availability and do not fully capture the distributed and heterogeneous nature of real-world traffic systems. To address these challenges, this study proposes FedLLM (Federated LLM), a privacy-preserving and distributed framework for explainable multi-horizon short-term traffic flow prediction (15-60 minutes). The framework introduces four key contributions: 1) a Composite Selection Score (CSS) for data-driven freeway selection that captures structural diversity across traffic regions 2) a domain-adapted LLM fine-tuned on structured traffic prompts encoding spatial, temporal, and statistical context 3) FedLLM framework, that enables collaborative training across heterogeneous clients while exchanging only lightweight LoRA adapter parameters, 4) a structured prompt representation that supports contextual reasoning and cross-region generalization. The FedLLM design allows each client to learn from local traffic patterns while contributing to a shared global model through efficient parameter exchange, reducing communication overhead and keeping data private. This setup supports learning under non-IID traffic distributions. Experimental results show that FedLLM achieves improved predictive performance over centralized baselines, while producing structured and explainable outputs. These findings highlight the potential of combining FL with domain-adapted LLMs for scalable, privacy-aware, and explainable traffic prediction.

</details>


### [108] [FRIGID: Scaling Diffusion-Based Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra at Training and Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16648)
*Montgomery Bohde,Hongxuan Liu,Mrunali Manjrekar,Magdalena Lederbauer,Shuiwang Ji,Runzhong Wang,Connor W. Coley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRIGID是一个基于扩散语言模型的框架，通过指纹表示和化学式从质谱生成分子结构，并通过推理时缩放显著提升准确率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从质谱数据中从头解析分子结构的挑战，传统方法准确率有限，需要更有效的分子结构生成方法

Method: 开发基于扩散的语言模型，通过中间指纹表示和确定的化学式从质谱生成分子结构；引入前向碎片化模型实现推理时缩放，通过识别不一致碎片并进行针对性重掩码和去噪来优化结构

Result: 在MassSpecGym基准测试中达到超过18%的Top-1准确率，在NPLIB1上将领先方法的Top-1准确率提高了三倍；显示推理时计算增加时性能呈对数线性缩放

Conclusion: FRIGID为从头结构解析提供了有前景的新方向，推理时缩放显著提升性能，代码已开源

Abstract: In this work, we present FRIGID, a framework with a novel diffusion language model that generates molecular structures conditioned on mass spectra via intermediate fingerprint representations and determined chemical formulae, training at the scale of hundreds of millions of unlabeled structures. We then demonstrate how forward fragmentation models enable inference-time scaling by identifying spectrum-inconsistent fragments and refining them through targeted remasking and denoising. While FRIGID already achieves strong performance with its diffusion base, inference-time scaling significantly improves its accuracy, surpassing 18% Top-1 accuracy on the challenging MassSpecGym benchmark and tripling the Top-1 accuracy of the leading methods on NPLIB1. Further empirical analyses show that FRIGID exhibits log-linear performance scaling with increasing inference-time compute, opening a promising new direction for continued improvements in de novo structural elucidation. FRIGID code is publicly available at https://github.com/coleygroup/FRIGID

</details>


### [109] [UniCon: Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16678)
*Hangke Sui,Yuqing Wang,Minh N Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniCon提出了一种基于核方法的统一对比对齐框架，通过闭式全局解替代随机优化，显著提升训练效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于对比目标的多模态模型训练速度慢，依赖长时间随机优化，需要更高效的训练方法

Method: 提出UniCon统一框架，引入对比相似度权重矩阵S(γ)，在再生核希尔伯特空间视角下提供闭式全局解，统一线性和非线性编码器以及一对一和多对多对齐

Result: 在合成、单模态、多模态和零样本任务上验证，UniCon在保持通用性和强经验性能的同时实现显著效率提升

Conclusion: UniCon通过核化视角统一对比对齐，揭示了与谱方法的联系，为高效对比学习提供了理论框架

Abstract: Contrastive objectives power state-of-the-art multimodal models, but their training remains slow, relying on long stochastic optimization. We propose a Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels (UniCon), which spans linear and nonlinear encoders as well as one-to-one and many-to-many alignments. At its core, UniCon introduces the contrastive similarity weight matrix $S(γ)$, which enables closed-form global solutions that provably replace minibatch back-propagation with exact updates. Through the lens of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS), UniCon provides a kernelized perspective that unifies contrastive alignment and reveals its connection to spectral methods. To validate the theory, we conduct experiments on synthetic, unimodal, multimodal, and zero-shot tasks, demonstrating that UniCon achieves substantial efficiency gains while preserving generality and strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [110] [Graph Transformer-Based Pathway Embedding for Cancer Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16685)
*Koushik Howlader,Md Tauhidul Islam,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH提出了一种基于调制的基因嵌入策略，从共享的基因基础表征出发，通过患者特异性变异信号动态调整，结合图Transformer建模通路交互，显著提升了癌症转移预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有癌症进展预测模型在基因编码方面存在局限：分层模型直接映射原始分子输入，集成框架依赖简单的统计聚合，都未能显式学习基因的共享基础表征，限制了通路嵌入的表达能力和生物学准确性。

Method: 提出PATH策略：1) 为每个基因学习共享基础嵌入，保持跨人群的稳定生物学身份；2) 使用患者特异性CNV和突变信号动态调制基础嵌入；3) 集成到图Transformer框架中，通过通路引导注意力建模生物连接通路间的交互。

Result: 在泛癌转移预测中，PATH达到0.8766的F1分数，比当前SOTA多组学基准提升8.8%。不仅预测准确性优越，还能识别生物学意义通路，揭示疾病状态特异性通路重连，提供癌症进展中通路-通路交互演化的新见解。

Conclusion: PATH通过共享基因基础表征与患者特异性调制的结合，实现了更准确、更具生物学解释性的癌症进展预测，为理解癌症异质性和通路动态交互提供了新框架。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of cancer progression remains a challenge due to the high heterogeneity of molecular omics data across patients. While biologically informed models have improved the interpretability of these predictions, a persistent limitation lies in how they encode individual genes to construct pathway representations. Existing hierarchical models typically derive gene features by directly mapping raw molecular inputs, whereas integration frameworks often rely on simple statistical aggregations of patient-level signals. These approaches often fail to explicitly learn a shared base representation for each gene, thereby limiting the expressiveness and biological accuracy of downstream pathway embeddings. To address this, we introduce PATH, a modulation-based, patient-conditioned gene embedding strategy. PATH represents a paradigm shift by starting from a shared base embedding for each gene, preserving a stable biological identity across the population, and then dynamically adapting it using patient-specific copy number variation (CNV) and mutation signals. This allows the model to capture subtle individual molecular variations while maintaining a consistent latent understanding of the gene itself. We integrate PATH into a graph transformer framework that models interactions among biologically connected pathways through pathway-guided attention. Across pancancer metastasis prediction, PATH achieves an F1 score of 0.8766, representing an 8.8 percent improvement over the current SOTA multi-omics benchmarks. Beyond superior predictive accuracy, our approach identifies biologically meaningful pathways and, crucially, reveals disease-state-specific pathway rewiring, offering new insights into the evolving pathway-pathway interactions that drive cancer progression.

</details>


### [111] [Chronax: A Jax Library for Univariate Statistical Forecasting and Conformal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16719)
*Xan Carey,Yash Deshmukh,Aileen Huang,Sunit Jadhav,Omkar Tekawade,Lorraine Yang,Anvesha Tiwary,Gerardo Riano,Amy Greenwald,Denizalp Goktas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronax是一个基于JAX的原生时间序列预测库，通过函数式编程范式重构预测系统，实现可组合性、大规模并行化和与现代化可微分工作流的无缝集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列预测库依赖于传统的Python数值栈，采用解释器驱动执行和面向对象抽象，限制了可组合性、大规模并行化以及与现代化可微分和加速器导向工作流的集成。随着预测任务涉及大规模异构时间序列数据、不规则协变量和频繁重训练，对可扩展性和执行效率提出了新要求。

Method: Chronax采用JAX的函数式编程范式，将预处理、建模和多步预测表示为纯JAX函数。利用JAX的纯函数和程序转换特性（如即时编译和自动向量化），实现跨CPU、GPU和TPU的端到端优化。支持可组合变换和加速器就绪执行。

Result: Chronax实现了可扩展的多序列预测、模型无关的符合不确定性量化，并能与现代化机器学习和科学计算管道无缝集成。通过函数式抽象，提升了预测系统的可组合性、并行化能力和执行效率。

Conclusion: Chronax将JAX的函数式编程范式引入时间序列预测领域，为处理大规模异构时间序列数据提供了现代化解决方案，解决了传统预测库在可组合性、并行化和与现代ML工作流集成方面的局限性。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is central to many scientific and industrial domains, such as energy systems, climate modeling, finance, and retail. While forecasting methods have evolved from classical statistical models to automated, and neural approaches, the surrounding software ecosystem remains anchored to the traditional Python numerical stack. Existing libraries rely on interpreter-driven execution and object-oriented abstractions, limiting composability, large-scale parallelism, and integration with modern differentiable and accelerator-oriented workflows. Meanwhile, today's forecasting increasingly involves large collections of heterogeneous time series data, irregular covariates, and frequent retraining, placing new demands on scalability and execution efficiency. JAX offers an alternative paradigm to traditional stateful numerical computation frameworks based on pure functions and program transformations such as just-in-time compilation and automatic vectorization, enabling end-to-end optimization across CPUs, GPUs, and TPUs. However, this modern paradigm has not yet been fully incorporated into the design of forecasting systems. We introduce Chronax, a JAX-native time-series forecasting library that rethinks forecasting abstractions around functional purity, composable transformations, and accelerator-ready execution. By representing preprocessing, modeling, and multi-horizon prediction as pure JAX functions, Chronax enables scalable multi-series forecasting, model-agnostic conformal uncertainty quantification, and seamless integration with modern machine learning and scientific computing pipelines.

</details>


### [112] [Late Fusion Neural Operators for Extrapolation Across Parameter Space in Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16721)
*Eva van Tegelen,Taniya Kapoor,George A. K. van Voorn,Peter van Heijster,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Late Fusion Neural Operator架构，通过解耦状态动力学与参数效应学习，在四个PDE基准测试中显著优于现有方法，实现72.9%域内和71.8%域外RMSE平均降低


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学和工程应用中，物理参数变化导致训练和预测机制间的分布偏移，使外推成为核心挑战。现有神经算子模型将状态和参数表示纠缠在一起，限制了其在未见参数机制下的泛化能力

Method: 提出Late Fusion Neural Operator架构，解耦学习状态动力学与参数效应：使用神经算子学习潜在状态表示，结合稀疏回归以结构化方式融入参数信息

Result: 在四个PDE基准测试（平流、Burgers、1D和2D反应扩散方程）中，该方法一致优于Fourier Neural Operator和CAPE-FNO。在所有实验中均获得最佳性能，相比次优方法平均RMSE降低72.9%（域内）和71.8%（域外）

Conclusion: Late Fusion Neural Operator通过解耦状态动力学与参数效应学习，显著提升了神经算子在域内和域外参数机制下的泛化能力，为PDE系统预测提供了更稳健的解决方案
